Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hyperbolic operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 379. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 803 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by hyperbolic generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 803. QED.
